They are never compared.\n */\nexport const ASSERTED = Object.freeze(['contractual', 'compatibility_required']);\n\n/** The observable surfaces a case may exercise. */\nexport const SURFACES = Object.freeze([\n  'sdk', 'http', 'schema', 'action-metadata', 'app-inspect', 'solution-plan', 'audit', 'events', 'trace', 'storage', 'admin',\n]);\n\nexport const CATEGORIES = Object.freeze([\n  'enrichment', 'signals', 'scoring', 'routing', 'assignment', 'lifecycle',\n  'audit-events-trace', 'storage-restart', 'hostile-input', 'architecture', 'helpers',\n]);\n\n/**\n * One observation.\n *\n * @param {{id: string, category: string, classification: string, surface: string,\n *   observed: unknown, note?: string|null}} input\n */\nexport function observation({ id, category, classification, surface, observed, note = null, allowEmpty = false }) {\n  if (!CATEGORIES.includes(category)) throw new Error(`characterization: unknown category \"${category}\" for ${id}`);\n  if (!CLASSIFICATIONS.includes(classification)) throw new Error(`characterization: unknown classification \"${classification}\" for ${id}`);\n  if (!SURFACES.includes(surface)) throw new Error(`characterization: unknown surface \"${surface}\" for ${id}`);\n  if (classification === 'defect_candidate' && !note) {\n    throw new Error(`characterization: ${id} is a defect candidate with no explanation — the note IS the finding`);\n  }\n  // A case that observed nothing asserts nothing and passes forever. It has\n  // happened twice while this harness was being built — once reading a field\n  // the action result does not return, once reading `action.inputs` where the\n  // published field is `action.input` — and both times the case sat green while\n  // proving absolutely nothing. An empty value is now an error unless the\n  // emptiness is itself the finding and is declared.\n  if (ASSERTED.includes(classification) && isEmpty(observed) && !allowEmpty) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `characterization: ${id} observed nothing (${JSON.stringify(observed)}). `\n      + 'A case that observes nothing asserts nothing. Fix the read, or pass allowEmpty with a note saying why empty is the answer.',\n    );\n  }\n  if (allowEmpty && !note) throw new Error(`characterization: ${id} allows an empty observation without saying why`);\n  return { id, category, classification, surface, observed, note };\n}\n\n/** @param {unknown} value */\nfunction isEmpty(value) {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return true;\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.length === 0;\n  if (typeof value === 'object') return Object.keys(value).length === 0;\n  return false;\n}\n\n/**\n * Canonical JSON: sorted keys, so a baseline diff shows a changed *value* and\n * never a changed key order.\n *\n * @param {unknown} value\n * @returns {string}\n */\nexport function canonical(value) {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null';\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonical).join(',')}]`;\n  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();\n  return `{${keys.map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical(value[key])}`).join(',')}}`;\n}\n\n/** Any generated identifier, wherever it appears — including inside a string. */\nexport const UUID_RE = /[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}/gi;\n\n/**\n * Replace generated identifiers with **positional** tokens, preserving identity\n * relationships.\n *\n * The first version mapped every UUID to a single `<id>`. That made the values\n * deterministic and destroyed the one property worth keeping: two *different*\n * ids became the same token, so a swapped, reused or cross-wired identifier was\n * undetectable — a run whose signal pointed at the wrong lead would have\n * compared equal to a correct one.\n *\n * A positional mapping keeps both halves of the invariant:\n *\n * ```text\n * same generated id   ->  same token      (deterministic across runs)\n * different ids       ->  different tokens (a swap is a difference)\n * ```\n *\n * The mapping is per-call, so the token a given id receives depends only on the\n * order ids first appear in that value — which is itself part of the shape being\n * frozen. Provider, model, policy and version identities are **never**\n * normalized: they are not generated, and they are the decision's identity.\n *\n * @param {unknown} value @param {Map<string, string>} [mapping]\n */\nexport function normalizeIds(value, mapping = new Map()) {\n  const token = (id) => {\n    const key = id.toLowerCase();\n    if (!mapping.has(key)) mapping.set(key, `<id:${mapping.size + 1}>`);\n    return mapping.get(key);\n  };\n  const walk = (input) => {\n    if (typeof input === 'string') return input.replace(UUID_RE, token);\n    if (Array.isArray(input)) return input.map(walk);\n    if (input && typeof input === 'object') {\n      const out = {};\n      for (const key of Object.keys(input).sort()) out[key] = walk(input[key]);\n      return out;\n    }\n    return input;\n  };\n  return walk(value);\n}\n\n/**\n * The same value with every object key sorted, recursively.\n *\n * The comparison is already key-order-insensitive, so this changes no verdict.\n * It makes the *file* byte-reproducible, which is a different and equally\n * important property: `audit.counts-by-action` and `scale.total-distribution`\n * are built by reduction, so their key order follows runtime iteration order and\n * two regenerations of identical behaviour produced two different files. A\n * baseline whose diff is noise is a baseline nobody reads, and the whole\n * mechanism depends on somebody reading the diff.\n *\n * @param {unknown} value\n */\nexport function sortKeysDeep(value) {\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(sortKeysDeep);\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;\n  const out = {};\n  for (const key of Object.keys(value).sort()) out[key] = sortKeysDeep(value[key]);\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** @param {unknown} value */\nexport function digest(value) {\n  return createHash('sha256').update(canonical(value)).digest('hex');\n}\n\n/**\n * Everything a baseline promises *not* to cover. Written into the document, so\n * a reader who finds a gap after the extraction can see it was declared rather\n * than discovered.\n */\nexport const LIMITATIONS = Object.freeze([\n  ['DETERMINISTIC_FIXTURES_ONLY', 'every provider is a checked-in deterministic fixture. No network call is made, so nothing here characterizes a real provider\\'s latency, error shape or data quality'],\n  ['INCIDENTAL_NOT_ASSERTED', 'observations classified incidental are recorded and not compared. Row ordering nobody specified and internal id shapes are free to change; freezing them would block the refactor this baseline exists to enable'],\n  ['DEFECTS_NOT_FROZEN', 'observations classified defect_candidate are reproduced and documented but never asserted as must-stay. Each one is a recommendation for a separate pre-extraction fix'],\n  ['NOT_A_PERFORMANCE_BASELINE', 'no timing is captured. An extraction that preserves every value and doubles the latency passes this suite'],\n  ['CONCURRENCY_ONLY_WHERE_OBSERVABLE', 'races are characterized only where the current implementation exposes a deterministic outcome. Where it does not, the absence of a case is not a statement that concurrency is safe'],\n  ['ADMIN_SURFACE_NOT_MODELLED', 'Lead Intelligence contributes no Admin section today, so there is no Admin behaviour to freeze. If one is added before the extraction, this baseline does not cover it'],\n  ['SINGLE_ATTACHMENT_SHAPE', 'a baseline records how Intelligence was attached when it was generated. Comparing baselines generated under different attachment shapes is a comparison of two different applications, and the contract carries the shape so that cannot happen silently'],\n]);\n\n/**\n * @param {{domain: string, attachment: string, source: Record<string, string>,\n *   observations: ReturnType<typeof observation>[]}} input\n */\nexport function buildBaseline({ domain, attachment, source, observations }) {\n  const sorted = [...observations].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));\n  const seen = new Set();\n  for (const entry of sorted) {\n    if (seen.has(entry.id)) throw new Error(`characterization: duplicate observation id \"${entry.id}\"`);\n    seen.add(entry.id);\n  }\n  const baseline = {\n    legacyCharacterizationContract: LEGACY_CHARACTERIZATION_CONTRACT,\n    domain,\n    // The attachment shape and the source fingerprints together answer \"is this\n    // baseline still about the code in front of me\". They are what makes a\n    // deliberate pre-extraction behaviour change *stale* the baseline instead of\n    // silently passing.\n    attachment,\n    source,\n    counts: CLASSIFICATIONS.reduce((totals, classification) => ({\n      ...totals,\n      [classification]: sorted.filter((entry) => entry.classification === classification).length,\n    }), {}),\n    observations: sorted,\n    limitations: LIMITATIONS.map(([code, message]) => ({ code, message })),\n  };\n  // Over the asserted observations only: an incidental value changing must not\n  // move the fingerprint, or \"incidental\" would mean nothing.\n  baseline.fingerprint = digest(sorted\n    .filter((entry) => ASSERTED.includes(entry.classification))\n    .map((entry) => ({ id: entry.id, classification: entry.classification, observed: entry.observed })));\n  return baseline;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compare a fresh run against a checked-in baseline.\n *\n * Three kinds of difference, and they are not the same kind of problem:\n * a **changed** asserted value is a behaviour change; a **missing** observation\n * means a case stopped running, which hides a behaviour change; and an **added**\n * one means the suite grew, which needs a deliberate regeneration rather than a\n * silent pass.\n *\n * @param {any} baseline @param {ReturnType<typeof observation>[]} fresh\n */\nexport function compareToBaseline(baseline, fresh) {\n  const freshById = new Map(fresh.map((entry) => [entry.id, entry]));\n  const baseById = new Map((baseline.observations ?? []).map((entry) => [entry.id, entry]));\n\n  const changed = [];\n  const missing = [];\n  const added = [];\n  const reclassified = [];\n\n  for (const [id, before] of baseById) {\n    const after = freshById.get(id);\n    if (!after) { missing.push(id); continue; }\n    if (after.classification !== before.classification) {\n      reclassified.push({ id, from: before.classification, to: after.classification });\n    }\n    if (!ASSERTED.includes(before.classification)) continue;\n    if (canonical(after.observed) !== canonical(before.observed)) {\n      changed.push({ id, classification: before.classification, before: before.observed, after: after.observed });\n    }\n  }\n  for (const id of freshById.keys()) if (!baseById.has(id)) added.push(id);\n\n  return {\n    ok: changed.length === 0 && missing.length === 0 && added.length === 0 && reclassified.length === 0,\n    changed, missing: missing.sort(), added: added.sort(), reclassified,\n  };\n}\n","numLines":273,"startLine":1,"totalLines":273}}}

['empty-array', []],\n    ['null', null],\n    ['scalar-string', 'a'],\n    ['scalar-number', 42],\n    ['scalar-boolean', true],\n    ['flat', { b: 1, a: 2 }],\n    ['key-order-swapped', { a: 2, b: 1 }],\n    ['nested', { a: { c: [1, 2, { d: 'e' }] }, b: null }],\n    ['array-order', [1, 2, 3]],\n    ['array-order-reversed', [3, 2, 1]],\n    ['unicode', { 'ключ': 'значение', emoji: 'x' }],\n    ['deep', { a: { b: { c: { d: { e: { f: 1 } } } } } }],\n  ];\n  for (const [label, value] of SHAPES) {\n    record(observation({\n      id: `helpers.computeDefinitionFingerprint.${label}`,\n      category: 'helpers',\n      classification: 'compatibility_required',\n      surface: 'sdk',\n      observed: computeDefinitionFingerprint(value),\n      note: 'Pinned so the recommended mechanical move of this helper out of an Intelligence file can be proved to change nothing.',\n    }));\n  }\n  // Key order must not matter, and array order must.\n  record(observation({\n    id: 'helpers.computeDefinitionFingerprint.canonicality',\n    category: 'helpers',\n    classification: 'compatibility_required',\n    surface: 'sdk',\n    observed: {\n      keyOrderIrrelevant: computeDefinitionFingerprint({ a: 1, b: 2 }) === computeDefinitionFingerprint({ b: 2, a: 1 }),\n      arrayOrderSignificant: computeDefinitionFingerprint([1, 2]) !== computeDefinitionFingerprint([2, 1]),\n    },\n    note: 'The two properties every consumer of a declared-definition fingerprint depends on.',\n  }));\n\n  const resolved = await withTimeout(Promise.resolve('value'), 1_000, 'probe').then(\n    (value) => ({ outcome: 'resolved', value }), (error) => ({ outcome: 'threw', message: error.message }));\n  const rejected = await withTimeout(Promise.reject(new Error('inner failure')), 1_000, 'probe').then(\n    () => ({ outcome: 'resolved' }), (error) => ({ outcome: 'threw', message: error.message }));\n  const timedOut = await withTimeout(new Promise(() => {}), 20, 'probe').then(\n    () => ({ outcome: 'resolved' }), (error) => ({ outcome: 'threw', message: error.message }));\n  record(observation({\n    id: 'helpers.withTimeout.outcomes',\n    category: 'helpers',\n    classification: 'compatibility_required',\n    surface: 'sdk',\n    observed: { resolved, rejected, timedOut },\n    note: 'Pinned for the same reason: the move must not change which error a caller sees.',\n  }));\n}\n\n/**\n * Every consumer of `app.intelligence`, measured from source rather than\n * remembered — and measured in *code*, which is a different claim from\n * measured in *text*. Evidence for the decisions recorded in ADR-021 and\n * ADR-022; nothing here implements them.\n *\n * @param {(entry: any) => void} record\n */\nexport async function runArchitectureEvidence(record, rootDir = repoRoot) {\n  const { observation } = await import('./characterization-contract.mjs');\n  const { readsProperty, importsFrom, SCANNER_LIMITS } = await import('./source-scan.mjs');\n\n  // Every checked-in source file, minus the harness's own tests. Reading the\n  // files is what makes the difference: the previous version asked `git grep`\n  // for a substring, which counted a comment as a consumer and missed\n  // `app['intelligence']` entirely. '\n      + `Scanner limits: ${SCANNER_LIMITS.join('; ')}.`,\n  }));\n\n  const internalImporters = sourceFiles\n    .filter((file) => importsFrom(read(file), /intelligence-(registry|actions)\\.js$/).length > 0).sort();\n  record(observation({\n    id: 'architecture.intelligence-internal-importers',\n    category: 'architecture',\n    classification: 'pre_extraction_evidence',\n    surface: 'sdk',\n    observed: internalImporters,\n    note: 'Blocker 1 evidence: every file with a real import statement naming an Intelligence internal.',\n  }));\n\n  const slotDependants = sourceFiles\n    .filter((file) => /intelligence\\/generated/.test(stripCode(read(file)))).sort();\n  record(observation({\n    id: 'architecture.definition-registry-slot',\n    category: 'architecture',\n    classification: 'pre_extraction_evidence',\n    surface: 'app-inspect',\n    observed: slotDependants,\n    note: 'Blocker 3 evidence: who depends on the fixed project-owned definition slot, in code.',\n  }));\n}\n\n/** The slot is referenced as a path string, so only comments are stripped. */\nfunction stripCode(source) {\n  return source.replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, ' ').replace(/(^|[^:])\\/\\/[^\\n]*/g, '$1 ');\n}\n\n/** Run everything and assemble the document. */\nexport async function generateBaseline(t) {\n  /** @type {any[]} */\n  const observations = [];\n  const record = (entry) => observations.push(entry);\n\n  await runIntelligenceCases(t, record);\n  await runRestartCases(t, record);\n  await runScaleCases(t, record);\n  await runHelperCases(record);\n  await runArchitectureEvidence(record);\n\n  return buildBaseline({\n    domain: 'lead-intelligence',\n    attachment: ATTACHMENT,\n    source: sourceFingerprints(),\n    observations,\n  });\n}\n","numLines":201,"startLine":1,"totalLines":201}}}

It moves no code, changes no\n20\t * architecture, moves no helper, replaces no ambient field and adds no seam.\n21\t *\n22\t * The one thing it must never become is a suite that freezes bugs. Every\n23\t * observation is classified, and only `contractual` and\n24\t * `compatibility_required` are compared — `incidental` is recorded so a change\n25\t * is visible without forbidding it, and `defect_candidate` is reproduced and\n26\t * documented so nobody rediscovers it mid-extraction.\n27\t */\n28\t\n29\tconst repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL('../..', import.meta.url));\n30\tconst BASELINE = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'tests/characterization/intelligence-baseline.json'), 'utf8'));\n31\t\n32\t/** Generated once: booting real projects for every test would be minutes of nothing new. */\n33\tlet fresh;\n34\ttest.before(async (t) => { fresh = await generateBaseline(t); }, { timeout: 600_000 });\n35\t\n36\ttest('the checked-in baseline is a valid contract document', () => {\n37\t  assert.equal(BASELINE.legacyCharacterizationContract, LEGACY_CHARACTERIZATION_CONTRACT);\n38\t  assert.equal(BASELINE.domain, 'lead-intelligence');\n39\t  assert.equal(BASELINE.attachment, ATTACHMENT);\n40\t  assert.ok(BASELINE.observations.length > 100, 'a thin baseline proves thin things');\n41\t\n42\t  for (const entry of BASELINE.observations) {\n43\t    assert.ok(CLASSIFICATIONS.includes(entry.classification), `${entry.id}: ${entry.classification}`);\n44\t    if (entry.classification === 'defect_candidate') {\n45\t      assert.ok(entry.note, `${entry.id}: a defect candidate without an explanation is not a finding`);\n46\t    }\n47\t  }\n48\t  // No absolute path, no secret, no source body, no wall clock.\n49\t  const text = JSON.stringify(BASELINE);\n50\t  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /\\/home\\/|\\/Users\\/|C:\\\\\\\\/, 'no absolute path');\n51\t  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /-----BEGIN|password|secret_key/i, 'no credential-shaped text');\n52\t  assert.ok(text.length < 2_000_000, 'a baseline nobody can read is a baseline nobody reviews');\n53\t});\n54\t\n55\ttest('the current behaviour matches the frozen baseline', () => {\n56\t  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, fresh.observations);\n57\t  assert.deepEqual(comparison.changed, [], 'an asserted value moved: that is a behaviour change, not a baseline update');\n58\t  assert.deepEqual(comparison.missing, [], 'a case stopped running, which would hide a behaviour change');\n59\t  assert.deepEqual(comparison.added, [], 'the suite grew; regenerate the baseline deliberately');\n60\t  assert.deepEqual(comparison.reclassified, [], 'an observation changed classification without review');\n61\t  assert.equal(fresh.fingerprint, BASELINE.fingerprint);\n62\t});\n63\t\n64\ttest('the baseline is stale when behaviour-bearing source changes', () => {\n65\t  // Not a git SHA: a baseline must go stale when the source that decides\n66\t  // behaviour changes, and a SHA moves for a typo in a README.\n67\t  assert.deepEqual(sourceFingerprints(repoRoot), BASELINE.source,\n68\t    'a source file that decides Lead Intelligence behaviour changed — regenerate the baseline deliberately '\n69\t    + 'with `npm run characterize:intelligence` and review the diff');\n70\t\n71\t  for (const [file, digest] of Object.entries(BASELINE.source)) {\n72\t    assert.notEqual(digest, 'absent', `${file} is recorded as absent; the baseline was generated against a different tree`);\n73\t    assert.match(digest, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);\n74\t  }\n75\t});\n76\t\n77\ttest('generation is separate from verification, and never runs during verify', () => {\n78\t  const script = readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'scripts/characterize-intelligence.mjs'), 'utf8');\n79\t  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));\n80\t  // A harness that refreshed its own baseline during `verify` would turn every\n81\t  // behaviour change into a silent update — the one thing it must never do.\n82\t  assert.doesNotMatch(String(manifest.scripts.verify), /characterize/);\n83\t  assert.match(String(manifest.scripts['characterize:intelligence']), /characterize-intelligence/);\n84\t  assert.match(script, /intelligence-baseline\\.json/);\n85\t});\n86\t\n87\ttest('incidental observations are recorded and never asserted', () => {\n88\t  const incidental = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.classification === 'incidental');\n89\t  assert.ok(incidental.length > 0, 'nothing is purely incidental? that is a classification nobody used honestly');\n90\t\n91\t  // Changing an incidental value must not move the fingerprint. Freezing row\n92\t  // ordering is how a characterization suite becomes the thing that blocks the\n93\t  // refactor it was built to enable.\n94\t  const mutated = BASELINE.observations.map((entry) => (entry.classification === 'incidental'\n95\t    ? { ...entry, observed: ['completely', 'different', 'order'] } : entry));\n96\t  const rebuilt = buildBaseline({\n97\t    domain: BASELINE.domain, attachment: BASELINE.attachment, source: BASELINE.source, observations: mutated,\n98\t  });\n99\t  assert.equal(rebuilt.fingerprint, BASELINE.fingerprint);\n100\t  assert.deepEqual(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated).changed, []);\n101\t});\n102\t\n103\ttest('defect candidates are reproduced, documented and never frozen as contract', () => {\n104\t  const defects = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.classification === 'defect_candidate');\n105\t  assert.ok(defects.length > 0);\n106\t  for (const entry of defects) {\n107\t    assert.ok(!ASSERTED.includes(entry.classification), `${entry.id} must not be asserted as must-stay`);\n108\t    assert.match(entry.note, /[Rr]ecommend/, `${entry.id}: a defect candidate names the fix it recommends`);\n109\t  }\n110\t  // The two found today, so a reader of the diff knows what they are.\n111\t  assert.deepEqual(defects.map((entry) => entry.id).sort(), [\n112\t    'hostile-input.record-signal.control',\n113\t    'hostile-input.record-signal.oversized',\n114\t  ]);\n115\t  // And changing one must not fail the suite: they are not the contract.\n116\t  const mutated = BASELINE.observations.map((entry) => (entry.classification === 'defect_candidate'\n117\t    ? { ...entry, observed: { accepted: false, status: 422, stored: null } } : entry));\n118\t  assert.deepEqual(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated).changed, []);\n119\t});\n120\t\n121\ttest('more than 500 individual values are asserted, not a summary count', () => {\n122\t  const leaves = (value) => {\n123\t    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.length === 0 ? 1 : value.reduce((total, entry) => total + leaves(entry), 0);\n124\t    if (value && typeof value === 'object') {\n125\t      const keys = Object.keys(value);\n126\t      return keys.length === 0 ? 1 : keys.reduce((total, key) => total + leaves(value[key]), 0);\n127\t    }\n128\t    return 1;\n129\t  };\n130\t  const asserted = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => ASSERTED.includes(entry.classification));\n131\t  const total = asserted.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + leaves(entry.observed), 0);\n132\t  assert.ok(total > 500, `only ${total} exact values are asserted`);\n133\t});\n134\t\n135\ttest('generated ids are normalized positionally, so a swap is still a difference', () => {\n136\t  // A single `<id>` token made every UUID identical, which made the values\n137\t  // deterministic and destroyed the property worth keeping: a run whose signal\n138\t  // pointed at the wrong lead compared equal to a correct one.\n139\t  const a = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';\n140\t  const b = '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222';\n141\t  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: a }), { x: '<id:1>', y: '<id:1>' }, 'same id, same token');\n142\t  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: b }), { x: '<id:1>', y: '<id:2>' }, 'different ids, different tokens');\n143\t\n144\t  // The property that matters is the RELATIONSHIP, not the label. Two rows\n145\t  // pointing at one lead and two rows pointing at different leads must not\n146\t  // compare equal — that is how a signal attached to the wrong lead is caught.\n147\t  assert.notDeepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: a }), normalizeIds({ x: a, y: b }),\n148\t    'a broken relationship must be visible');\n149\t\n150\t  // And the limitation, stated rather than assumed: a *consistent* global\n151\t  // relabelling IS equivalent, because a generated id is arbitrary. test from 'node:test';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { join } from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport {\n  ASSERTED, CLASSIFICATIONS, LEGACY_CHARACTERIZATION_CONTRACT, buildBaseline, canonical,\n  compareToBaseline, normalizeIds, observation, sortKeysDeep,\n} from './characterization-contract.mjs';\nimport {\n  ATTACHMENT, BEHAVIOUR_BEARING_SOURCE, INTELLIGENCE_SOURCE, unownedIntelligenceSource,\n} from './intelligence-harness.mjs';\nimport { generateBaseline, sourceFingerprints } from './run-intelligence-characterization.mjs';\n\n/**\n * LA0 — the Lead Intelligence characterization gate.\n *\n * This suite freezes what Lead Intelligence *externally does* today, so the\n * extraction can be proved to change none of it. It moves no code, changes no\n * architecture, moves no helper, replaces no ambient field and adds no seam.\n *\n * The one thing it must never become is a suite that freezes bugs. Every\n * observation is classified, and only `contractual` and\n * `compatibility_required` are compared — `incidental` is recorded so a change\n * is visible without forbidding it, and `defect_candidate` is reproduced and\n * documented so nobody rediscovers it mid-extraction.\n */\n\nconst repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL('../..', import.meta.url));\nconst BASELINE = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'tests/characterization/intelligence-baseline.json'), 'utf8'));\n\n/** Generated once: booting real projects for every test would be minutes of nothing new. */\nlet fresh;\ntest.before(async (t) => { fresh = await generateBaseline(t); }, { timeout: 600_000 });\n\ntest('the checked-in baseline is a valid contract document', () => {\n  assert.equal(BASELINE.legacyCharacterizationContract, LEGACY_CHARACTERIZATION_CONTRACT);\n  assert.equal(BASELINE.domain, 'lead-intelligence');\n  assert.equal(BASELINE.attachment, ATTACHMENT);\n  assert.ok(BASELINE.observations.length > 100, 'a thin baseline proves thin things');\n\n  for (const entry of BASELINE.observations) {\n    assert.ok(CLASSIFICATIONS.includes(entry.classification), `${entry.id}: ${entry.classification}`);\n    if (entry.classification === 'defect_candidate') {\n      assert.ok(entry.note, `${entry.id}: a defect candidate without an explanation is not a finding`);\n    }\n  }\n  // No absolute path, no secret, no source body, no wall clock.\n  const text = JSON.stringify(BASELINE);\n  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /\\/home\\/|\\/Users\\/|C:\\\\\\\\/, 'no absolute path');\n  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /-----BEGIN|password|secret_key/i, 'no credential-shaped text');\n  assert.ok(text.length < 2_000_000, 'a baseline nobody can read is a baseline nobody reviews');\n});\n\ntest('the current behaviour matches the frozen baseline', () => {\n  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, fresh.observations);\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.changed, [], 'an asserted value moved: that is a behaviour change, not a baseline update');\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.missing, [], 'a case stopped running, which would hide a behaviour change');\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.added, [], 'the suite grew; regenerate the baseline deliberately');\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.reclassified, [], 'an observation changed classification without review');\n  assert.equal(fresh.fingerprint, BASELINE.fingerprint);\n});\n\ntest('the baseline is stale when behaviour-bearing source changes', () => {\n  // Not a git SHA: a baseline must go stale when the source that decides\n  // behaviour changes, and a SHA moves for a typo in a README.\n  assert.deepEqual(sourceFingerprints(repoRoot), BASELINE.source,\n    'a source file that decides Lead Intelligence behaviour changed — regenerate the baseline deliberately '\n    + 'with `npm run characterize:intelligence` and review the diff');\n\n  for (const [file, digest] of Object.entries(BASELINE.source)) {\n    assert.notEqual(digest, 'absent', `${file} is recorded as absent; the baseline was generated against a different tree`);\n    assert.match(digest, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('generation is separate from verification, and never runs during verify', () => {\n  const script = readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'scripts/characterize-intelligence.mjs'), 'utf8');\n  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));\n  // A harness that refreshed its own baseline during `verify` would turn every\n  // behaviour change into a silent update — the one thing it must never do.\n  assert.doesNotMatch(String(manifest.scripts.verify), /characterize/);\n  assert.match(String(manifest.scripts['characterize:intelligence']), /characterize-intelligence/);\n  assert.match(script, /intelligence-baseline\\.json/);\n});\n\ntest('incidental observations are recorded and never asserted', () => {\n  const incidental = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.classification === 'incidental');\n  assert.ok(incidental.length > 0, 'nothing is purely incidental? that is a classification nobody used honestly');\n\n  // Changing an incidental value must not move the fingerprint. Freezing row\n  // ordering is how a characterization suite becomes the thing that blocks the\n  // refactor it was built to enable.\n  const mutated = BASELINE.observations.map((entry) => (entry.classification === 'incidental'\n    ? { ...entry, observed: ['completely', 'different', 'order'] } : entry));\n  const rebuilt = buildBaseline({\n    domain: BASELINE.domain, attachment: BASELINE.attachment, source: BASELINE.source, observations: mutated,\n  });\n  assert.equal(rebuilt.fingerprint, BASELINE.fingerprint);\n  assert.deepEqual(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated).changed, []);\n});\n\ntest('defect candidates are reproduced, documented and never frozen as contract', () => {\n  const defects = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.classification === 'defect_candidate');\n  assert.ok(defects.length > 0);\n  for (const entry of defects) {\n    assert.ok(!ASSERTED.includes(entry.classification), `${entry.id} must not be asserted as must-stay`);\n    assert.match(entry.note, /[Rr]ecommend/, `${entry.id}: a defect candidate names the fix it recommends`);\n  }\n  // The two found today, so a reader of the diff knows what they are.\n  assert.deepEqual(defects.map((entry) => entry.id).sort(), [\n    'hostile-input.record-signal.control',\n    'hostile-input.record-signal.oversized',\n  ]);\n  // And changing one must not fail the suite: they are not the contract.\n  const mutated = BASELINE.observations.map((entry) => (entry.classification === 'defect_candidate'\n    ? { ...entry, observed: { accepted: false, status: 422, stored: null } } : entry));\n  assert.deepEqual(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated).changed, []);\n});\n\ntest('more than 500 individual values are asserted, not a summary count', () => {\n  const leaves = (value) => {\n    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.length === 0 ? 1 : value.reduce((total, entry) => total + leaves(entry), 0);\n    if (value && typeof value === 'object') {\n      const keys = Object.keys(value);\n      return keys.length === 0 ? 1 : keys.reduce((total, key) => total + leaves(value[key]), 0);\n    }\n    return 1;\n  };\n  const asserted = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => ASSERTED.includes(entry.classification));\n  const total = asserted.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + leaves(entry.observed), 0);\n  assert.ok(total > 500, `only ${total} exact values are asserted`);\n});\n\ntest('generated ids are normalized positionally, so a swap is still a difference', () => {\n  // A single `<id>` token made every UUID identical, which made the values\n  // deterministic and destroyed the property worth keeping: a run whose signal\n  // pointed at the wrong lead compared equal to a correct one.\n  const a = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';\n  const b = '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222';\n  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: a }), { x: '<id:1>', y: '<id:1>' }, 'same id, same token');\n  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: b }), { x: '<id:1>', y: '<id:2>' }, 'different ids, different tokens');\n\n  // The property that matters is the RELATIONSHIP, not the label. Two rows\n  // pointing at one lead and two rows pointing at different leads must not\n  // compare equal — that is how a signal attached to the wrong lead is caught.\n  assert.notDeepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: a }), normalizeIds({ x: a, y: b }),\n    'a broken relationship must be visible');\n\n  // And the limitation, stated rather than assumed: a *consistent* global\n  // relabelling IS equivalent, because a generated id is arbitrary. Two runs\n  // that differ only in which UUID the database happened to mint are the same\n  // run, and demanding otherwise would make every regeneration fail.\n  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ x: a, y: b }), normalizeIds({ x: b, y: a }));\n  // Embedded in a string, which is where the sourceKey carries one.\n  assert.equal(normalizeIds(`signal:${a}:x`).endsWith(':x'), true);\n  assert.equal(normalizeIds(`signal:${a}:x`), 'signal:<id:1>:x');\n  // Provider, model and version identities are never generated and never touched.\n  assert.deepEqual(normalizeIds({ model: 'b2b-saas-score', version: 2, fingerprint: 'a'.repeat(64) }),\n    { fingerprint: 'a'.repeat(64), model: 'b2b-saas-score', version: 2 });\n\n  const stored = BASELINE.observations.find((entry) => entry.id === 'signals.stored-shape');\n  assert.match(stored.observed[0].sourceKey, /^signal:<id:\\d+>:/);\n});\n\ntest('every behaviour-bearing file is owned by the digest, and the set cannot rot', () => {\n  // The first version listed eleven files and missed six that matter, including\n  // the action runtime, the starter's qualify/disqualify actions whose gating\n  // this suite freezes, and the HTTP server that publishes the schema block it\n  // freezes. Any of them could have changed observed behaviour without staling\n  // the baseline — the one failure a freshness mechanism cannot have.\n  for (const file of [\n    'packages/core/src/action-runtime.js',\n    'packages/app/src/create-app.js',\n    'apps/server/src/http-server.js',\n    'packages/sdk/src/index.js',\n    'examples/starters/b2b-lead-qualification/actions/qualify.js',\n    'examples/starters/b2b-lead-qualification/actions/disqualify.js',\n  ]) {\n    assert.ok(BEHAVIOUR_BEARING_SOURCE.includes(file), `${file} is not owned by the source digest`);\n  }\n  // And the guard: a future intelligence-*.js in the kernel, or a new\n  // Intelligence module manifest, cannot silently fall outside ownership.\n  assert.deepEqual(unownedIntelligenceSource(repoRoot), []);\n});\n\ntest('the wiring seam owns every path that knows where Intelligence lives', () => {\n  // The \"one file changes at extraction\" claim was false: the helper cases\n  // imported the internals directly and the evidence cases grepped for those\n  // paths, so extraction would have edited two files.\n  const dir = join(repoRoot, 'tests/characterization');\n  for (const file of readdirSync(dir)) {\n    // The harness owns the paths; this file asserts *about* them, so both are\n    // expected to mention them.\n    if (file === 'intelligence-harness.mjs' || file === 'intelligence-characterization.test.js') continue;\n    if (!/\\.(mjs|js)$/.test(file)) continue;\n    const source = readFileSync(join(dir, file), 'utf8');\n    assert.doesNotMatch(source, /['\"][^'\"]*intelligence-(actions|registry)[^'\"]*['\"]/,\n      `${file} reaches an Intelligence internal directly; it belongs in the harness`);\n    assert.doesNotMatch(source, /['\"][^'\"]*intelligence\\/generated[^'\"]*['\"]/, file);\n  }\n  // Post-extraction the seam points into the package. This assertion used to\n  // read /intelligence-registry/, which was the pre-extraction filename — a\n  // test that encoded the old topology and had to move with it.\n  assert.match(INTELLIGENCE_SOURCE.registry, /packages\\/intelligence\\/src\\/registry\\.js$/);\n  assert.match(INTELLIGENCE_SOURCE.entry, /packages\\/intelligence\\/src\\/index\\.js$/);\n  assert.equal(INTELLIGENCE_SOURCE.greps.length, 4);\n  // And the kernel must no longer contain the domain at all.\n  assert.equal(existsSync(join(repoRoot, 'packages/core/src/intelligence-registry.js')), false,\n    'the extraction is not done while the kernel still carries an Intelligence file');\n  assert.equal(existsSync(join(repoRoot, 'packages/intelligence/generated/index.js')), false,\n    'the fixed project-owned definition slot is replaced by composition, not kept alongside it');\n});\n\ntest('a case that observes nothing is refused at build time', () => {\n  const valid = { id: 'x', category: 'scoring', classification: 'contractual', surface: 'sdk' };\n  for (const empty of [[], {}, null, undefined, '']) {\n    assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, observed: empty }), /observed nothing/,\n      `${JSON.stringify(empty)} must be refused`);\n  }\n  // Unless the emptiness IS the answer and somebody said so.\n  assert.doesNotThrow(() => observation({ ...valid, observed: [], allowEmpty: true, note: 'no targets are configured in this fixture' }));\n  assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, observed: [], allowEmpty: true }), /without saying why/);\n  // A non-asserted classification may legitimately observe nothing.\n  assert.doesNotThrow(() => observation({ ...valid, classification: 'incidental', observed: [] }));\n});\n\ntest('legacy architecture facts are evidence, not a contract the extraction must preserve', () => {\n  const evidence = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.classification === 'pre_extraction_evidence');\n  // The ambient field, the ambient schema block, the internal importers and the\n  // fixed definition slot are exactly what the extraction exists to move.\n  // Asserting them would make LA0 fail a correct extraction.\n  assert.deepEqual(evidence.map((entry) => entry.id).sort(), [\n    'architecture.app-intelligence-consumers',\n    'architecture.app-intelligence-field-present',\n    'architecture.definition-registry-slot',\n    'architecture.intelligence-internal-importers',\n    'architecture.schema-intelligence-block-present',\n  ]);\n  assert.ok(!ASSERTED.includes('pre_extraction_evidence'));\n  const mutated = BASELINE.observations.map((entry) => (entry.classification === 'pre_extraction_evidence'\n    ? { ...entry, observed: { present: false } } : entry));\n  assert.deepEqual(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated).changed, [],\n    'the extraction must be free to change these');\n\n  // But consumer-visible facts about the same area stay contractual.\n  const kinds = BASELINE.observations.find((entry) => entry.id === 'architecture.definition-kinds-published');\n  assert.equal(kinds.classification, 'contractual');\n});\n\ntest('correctness evidence does not depend on a page bound', () => {\n  // `list()` defaults to 100 rows and caps at 500; `listWhere()` is the complete\n  // query. Reading evidence through an unbounded list() would truncate silently,\n  // and a truncated observation compared against a truncated baseline still\n  // passes while proving nothing.\n  const page = BASELINE.observations.find((entry) => entry.id === 'signals.beyond-the-default-page');\n  assert.equal(page.observed.written, 130);\n  assert.equal(page.observed.readBack, 130);\n  assert.equal(page.observed.complete, true);\n  assert.ok(page.observed.written > 100, 'the case must actually cross the default page');\n\n  const scored = BASELINE.observations.find((entry) => entry.id === 'scoring.signal-count-beyond-the-default-page');\n  assert.equal(scored.observed.signalCount, 130, 'the score must count every signal, not one page of them');\n\n  // No unbounded read remains in the cases.\n  const cases = readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'tests/characterization/intelligence-cases.mjs'), 'utf8');\n  assert.doesNotMatch(cases, /\\.list\\(\\)/, 'every read asks for an explicit bound');\n});\n\ntest('the 60-lead scenario is a scale scenario, not the >500 claim', () => {\n  const scale = BASELINE.observations.filter((entry) => entry.id.startsWith('scale.lead-'));\n  assert.equal(scale.length, 60);\n  // The \">500 exact reads\" bar is met by the total number of individually\n  // asserted VALUES across the whole baseline, which the test above counts.\n  // Sixty leads is a separate thing: breadth of input, not depth of assertion.\n  assert.ok(scale.length < 500, 'sixty is sixty; the >500 claim is about asserted values');\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// mutation sensitivity — a characterization suite that cannot fail is decoration\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Apply a mutation to a copy of the fresh observations and expect it to be caught. */\nfunction mutate(id, change) {\n  return fresh.observations.map((entry) => (entry.id === id || (typeof id === 'function' && id(entry))\n    ? { ...entry, observed: change(entry.observed) } : entry));\n}\n\ntest('LA0 fails when a score keeps its number but changes its fingerprint', () => {\n  const mutated = mutate('scoring.v1.identity', (observed) => ({ ...observed, fingerprint: 'f'.repeat(64) }));\n  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutated);\n  // The most important assertion in this file. A model returning the same\n  // number under a different fingerprint is a DIFFERENT decision, and a suite\n  // that only compared totals would call the extraction a success.\n  assert.equal(comparison.ok, false);\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.changed.map((entry) => entry.id), ['scoring.v1.identity']);\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when a provider fingerprint changes', () => {\n false);\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when the routing target set changes', () => {\n  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE,\n    mutate('routing.v1.target-set-evidence', (observed) => false);\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when an assignment stops being written', () => {\n  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutate('assignment.created-from-routing', () => []));\n assert.equal(comparison.ok, false);\n  assert.equal(comparison.changed[0].id, target.id);\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when schema or action metadata changes', () => {\n  for (const id of ['architecture.lead-actions-advertised', 'architecture.lead-managed-fields']) {\n    const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutate(id, (observed) => observed.slice(1)));\n    assert.equal(comparison.ok, false, `${id} must be caught`);\n  }\n  // Each part of an action's public contract separately: the route a consumer\n  // calls, the declared inputs, and the states it is allowed from.\n  for (const change of [\n    (observed) => ({ ...observed, path: '/api/somewhere/else' }),\n    (observed) => ({ ...observed, input: observed.input.slice(1) }),\n    (observed) => ({ assert.equal(comparison.ok, false, 'an action contract change must be caught');\n  }\n  // And the case must actually be capturing something: an empty observation\n  // asserts nothing, which is how 'helpers.withTimeout.outcomes']) {\n    const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, mutate(id, () => ({ changed: true })));\n    assert.equal(comparison.ok, false, `${id} must be caught`);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when a case silently stops running', () => {\n  const comparison = compareToBaseline(BASELINE, fresh.observations.filter((entry) => entry.id !== 'scoring.v1.total'));\n  assert.equal(comparison.ok, false);\n  assert.deepEqual(comparison.missing, ['scoring.v1.total']);\n});\n\ntest('LA0 fails when an observation is quietly reclassified', () => {\n  // Downgrading a contractual observation to incidental is how somebody makes a\n  // failing extraction pass. Two\n  // regenerations of identical behaviour produced two byte-different files\n  // until the writer sorted keys — and a baseline whose diff is noise is a\n  // baseline nobody reads.\n  const serialize = (value) => `${JSON.stringify(sortKeysDeep(value), null, 2)}\\n`;\n  assert.equal(serialize(fresh), readFileSync(join(repoRoot, 'tests/characterization/intelligence-baseline.json'), 'utf8'));\n  assert.equal(serialize({ b: 1, a: { d: 2, c: 3 } }), serialize({ a: { c: 3, d: 2 }, b: 1 }));\n});\n\ntest('the comparison is order-independent and key-order-independent', () => {\n  const shuffled = [...fresh.observations].reverse();\n  assert.equal(compareToBaseline(BASELINE, shuffled).ok, true);\n  assert.equal(canonical({ a: 1, b: 2 }), canonical({ b: 2, a: 1 }));\n});\n\ntest('an observation with an unknown classification, category or surface is refused', () => {\n  const valid = { id: 'x', category: 'scoring', classification: 'contractual', surface: 'sdk', observed: 1 };\n  assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, classification: 'probably-fine' }), /unknown classification/);\n  assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, category: 'vibes' }), /unknown category/);\n  assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, surface: 'telepathy' }), /unknown surface/);\n  assert.throws(() => observation({ ...valid, classification: 'defect_candidate' }), /the note IS the finding/);\n});\n","numLines":417,"startLine":1,"totalLines":417}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011Ce1drmhHsDsfANzzbcQKD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This repo already has a purpose-built mechanism for exactly this, so you don't need to invent a golden-master setup — you need to use it correctly.\n\n**What exists:** `tests/characterization/` implements \"LA0\", a characterization suite for Lead Intelligence (scoring, routing, assignment, enrichment). It records ~500+ individual values into a checked-in baseline (`tests/characterization/intelligence-baseline.json`), each tagged with a classification — only `contractual` and `compatibility_required` observations are compared; `incidental` (row order, etc.) is recorded but never asserted, so it won't block your restructuring. Scoring decisions specifically are pinned by id (`scoring.v1.identity`, `scoring.v1.total`, etc.) including the score's *fingerprint*, not just its number — so a model that returns the same score under a different derivation still fails the check. The baseline also carries content-hash digests of every behavior-bearing source file (`packages/intelligence/src/*`, its module manifests, plus a few kernel/wiring files), so it goes stale automatically if source it doesn't yet own changes.\n\n**How to use it for your restructure:**\n1. Before touching anything, run `npm test` (or `npm run verify`) once — this confirms the checked-in baseline is currently valid and not stale against today's code. That green run *is* your freeze.\n2. Do the internal restructuring.\n3. Run `npm test` again. If no scoring/routing/assignment decision changed, it passes silently.
